\displaystyle\varepsilon_{c\{a}\mathchoice{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\mathcal{E}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon_{c\{a}\mathchoice{\widehat{\Pi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\Pi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\Pi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\widehat{\Pi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\varepsilon_{c\{a}\delta^{c}\left(\mathcal{E}_{b\}}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi_{b\}}\right)+

(218)

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\varepsilon_{c\{a}\mathchoice{\zeta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\zeta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\zeta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\zeta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}+\Theta\right)\mathcal{H}_{ab}\;,

\dot{Q}+\widehat{p}+\widehat{\Pi}=-(\mu+p)\mathcal{A}-\delta_{a}\Pi^{a}-\left(\frac{3}{2}\phi+\mathcal{A}\right)\Pi-\left(\frac{4}{3}\Theta+\Sigma\right)Q\;.

(219)

\displaystyle\dot{Q}_{\overline{a}}+\widehat{\Pi}_{\overline{a}}+p_{a}=\frac{1}{2}Y_{a}-\delta^{b}\Pi_{ab}-\frac{3}{2}\Pi a_{a}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{4}{3}\Theta-\frac{1}{2}\Sigma\right)Q_{a}-(\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi)\mathcal{A}_{a}\;.

(220)

The equations containing only propagation contributions are

\widehat{\phi}=\frac{2\Theta^{2}}{9}+\frac{\Theta\Sigma}{3}+\delta_{a}a^{a}-\frac{2}{3}(\mu+\Lambda)-\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi-\Sigma^{2},

(221)

\widehat{\xi}=\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon_{ab}\delta^{a}a^{b},

(222)

\widehat{\zeta}_{\{ab\}}=\delta_{\{a}a_{b\}}+\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma_{ab}-\mathcal{E}_{ab}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi_{ab},

(223)

\displaystyle\widehat{V}_{\overline{a}}-\frac{2}{3}\widehat{W}_{\overline{a}}

\displaystyle=-\delta_{a}\delta_{b}\Sigma^{b}-\varepsilon_{bc}\delta_{a}\delta^{b}\Omega^{c}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\delta_{a}\phi

(224)

\displaystyle+2\left(\dot{\Sigma}-\frac{2}{3}\dot{\Theta}\right)\varepsilon_{ab}\Omega^{b}-\delta_{a}Q,

\widehat{\Sigma}_{\overline{a}}-\varepsilon_{ab}\widehat{\Omega}^{b}=\frac{1}{2}V_{a}+\frac{2}{3}W_{a}-\varepsilon_{ab}\delta^{b}\Omega-\delta^{b}\Sigma_{ab}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}a_{a}-Q_{a},

(225)

\widehat{\Omega}=-\delta_{a}\Omega^{a},

(226)

\widehat{\Sigma}_{\{ab\}}=\delta_{\{a}\Sigma_{b\}}-\varepsilon_{c\{a}\delta^{c}\Omega_{b\}}-\varepsilon_{c\{a}\mathchoice{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{b\}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\zeta_{ab},

(227)

\displaystyle\widehat{X}_{\overline{a}}+\frac{1}{2}\widehat{Y}_{\overline{a}}-\frac{1}{3}\widehat{\mu}_{\overline{a}}=-\delta_{a}\delta_{b}\left(\mathcal{E}^{b}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{b}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\delta_{a}\phi+\left(2\dot{\mathcal{E}}+\dot{\Pi}-\frac{2}{3}\dot{\mu}\right)\varepsilon_{ab}\Omega^{b}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{2}\Sigma-\frac{1}{3}\Theta\right)\delta_{a}Q\,,

(228)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{E}}_{\overline{a}}+\frac{1}{2}\widehat{\Pi}_{\overline{a}}=\frac{1}{2}X_{a}+\frac{1}{4}Y_{a}+\frac{1}{3}\mu_{a}-\delta^{b}\left(\mathcal{E}_{ab}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi_{ab}\right)-

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)a_{a}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\varepsilon_{ab}\mathcal{H}^{b}-\left(\frac{1}{3}\Theta+\frac{1}{4}\Sigma\right)Q_{a},

(229)

\widehat{\mathcal{H}}=-\delta_{a}\mathcal{H}^{a}-(\mu+p+3\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi)\Omega-\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon_{ab}\delta^{a}Q^{b},

(230)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{H}}_{\overline{a}}-\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon_{ab}\widehat{Q}^{b}=\frac{1}{2}\delta_{a}\mathcal{H}-\delta^{b}\mathcal{H}_{ab}-\left(\mu+p-\frac{3\mathcal{E}}{2}+\frac{1}{4}\Pi\right)\Omega_{a}

(231)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\varepsilon_{ab}\Sigma^{b}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\varepsilon_{ab}\left(\mathcal{E}^{b}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon_{ab}\delta^{b}Q\,.

Lastly, the constraints are

\delta_{a}\Omega^{a}+\varepsilon_{ab}\delta^{a}\Sigma^{b}=\mathcal{H}-3\Sigma\xi,

(232)

\frac{1}{2}\delta_{a}\phi-\varepsilon_{ab}\delta^{b}\xi-\delta^{b}\zeta_{ab}=\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}-\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\varepsilon_{ab}\Omega^{b}-\Sigma_{a}\right)-\mathcal{E}_{a}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi_{a},

(233)

V_{a}-\frac{2}{3}W_{a}+2\varepsilon_{ab}\delta^{b}\Omega+2\delta^{b}\Sigma_{ab}=-2\varepsilon_{ab}\mathcal{H}^{b}-Q_{a}\,.

(234)

Appendix E
Harmonic Expansion of Linearized Equations

Here the harmonic expansion of the first order equations given in section
D
is presented.

E.1
Even Parity

The evolution equations for the scalars are

\dot{\phi}^{S}=\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\frac{1}{2}\phi^{S}-\mathcal{A}^{S}\right)-\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\alpha^{V}+Q^{S},

(235)

\displaystyle\dot{Q}^{S}+a_{2}\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(p^{V}+Y^{V}\right)=-\left(\mu+p+\Pi\right)\mathcal{A}^{S}

(236)

\displaystyle-2a_{2}\overline{\Omega}^{V}\left(\dot{p}+\dot{\Pi}\right)-\frac{3}{2}\Pi\phi^{S}-\left(\frac{4}{3}\Theta+\Sigma\right)Q^{S}+\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}}\Pi^{V},

for the 2-vectors are

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\mathcal{H}}}^{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{V}\right)+\frac{3}{4}\left(\Sigma-\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}

(237)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{8}\left(2X^{V}-Y^{V}+\Sigma Q^{V}\right)-\frac{3\mathcal{E}}{2}\mathcal{A}^{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\left(\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)a^{V}-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{4a_{2}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T}\right),

\dot{\overline{\Omega}}^{V}=-\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}+\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}+\frac{1}{2a_{2}}\mathcal{A}^{S}-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\mathcal{A}^{V},

(238)

\displaystyle\dot{\mu}^{V}=\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\mu^{V}-\Theta\left(\mu^{V}+p^{V}\right)-(\mu+p)W^{V}

\displaystyle+\dot{\mu}\mathcal{A}^{V}-\frac{3}{2}\left(\Pi V^{V}+\Sigma Y^{V}\right)-\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}}Q^{S}+\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}Q^{V}\,,

(239)

\displaystyle\dot{X}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{Y}^{V}=2\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)X^{V}+\left(\dot{\mathcal{E}}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{\Pi}\right)\mathcal{A}^{V}

(240)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left(\mu+p-3\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)V^{V}-\frac{\Sigma}{2}\left(\mu^{V}+p^{V}\right)+\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}\Theta Y^{V}-\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{6}\Pi\right)W^{V}-\frac{1}{3}\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}}Q^{S}-\frac{1}{6}\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}Q^{V},

\displaystyle\dot{V}^{V}-\frac{2}{3}\dot{W}^{V}=\frac{1}{3}\left(\mu^{V}+3p^{V}\right)+\left(\dot{\Sigma}-\frac{2\dot{\Theta}}{3}+\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right)\mathcal{A}^{V}

(241)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\left(V^{V}-\frac{2}{3}W^{V}\right)-X^{V}+\frac{1}{2}Y^{V},

\dot{a}^{V}=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\alpha^{V}-\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\mathcal{A}^{V}+a^{V}\right)-\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}Q^{V},

(242)

\displaystyle\dot{W}^{V}

\displaystyle=\left(\dot{\Theta}-\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right)\mathcal{A}^{V}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\mathcal{A}^{S}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\mu^{V}+3p^{V}\right)

(243)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}-\Theta\right)W^{V}-3\Sigma V^{V},

\dot{\Sigma}^{V}=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\mathcal{A}^{V}+\frac{1}{2a_{2}}\mathcal{A}^{S}-\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}+\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma^{V}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\alpha^{V}-\mathcal{E}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{V},

(244)

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{\Pi}^{V}=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}-\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{4a_{1}}Q^{V}+\frac{3}{4}\left(\Sigma-\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{E}^{V}

(245)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4a_{2}}Q^{S}+\frac{1}{4}\left(3\mathcal{E}-2\mu-2p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\Sigma^{V}-\left(\frac{1}{6}\Theta+\frac{1}{8}\Sigma\right)\Pi^{V}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\alpha^{V}+\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{4a_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{3}{4}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\Omega^{V},

\displaystyle\dot{Q}^{V}+p^{V}+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\Pi^{V}=-\left(\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\mathcal{A}^{V}

(246)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}Y^{V}-\frac{3}{2}\Pi a^{V}-\left(\frac{4}{3}\Theta-\frac{1}{2}\Sigma\right)Q^{V}

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\Pi^{T},

and for the 2-tensors are

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\mathcal{H}}}^{T}

\displaystyle=-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T}\right)+\frac{1}{a_{2}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{V}\right)-

(247)

\displaystyle\left(\Theta+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}+\frac{3}{2}\left({\mathcal{E}}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\zeta^{T},

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{\Pi}^{T}=-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}(3\mathcal{E}+\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi)\Sigma^{T}

(248)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}-\left(\Theta+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\right)\mathcal{E}^{T}-\frac{1}{2a_{2}}Q^{V}-\frac{1}{6}\left(\Theta-\frac{3}{2}\Sigma\right)\Pi^{T},

\dot{\zeta}^{T}=\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\zeta^{T}+\frac{1}{a_{2}}\alpha^{V}+\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T},

(249)

\dot{\Sigma}^{T}=\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma^{T}+\frac{1}{a_{2}}\mathcal{A}^{V}-\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T}.

(250)

The constraint for the scalar is

\displaystyle\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\phi^{S}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3}\left(\Sigma+\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)W^{V}+\left(\frac{\Theta}{3}-2\Sigma\right)V^{V}

(251)

\displaystyle-\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}a^{V}-\frac{2}{3}\mu^{V}-X^{V}-\frac{1}{2}Y^{V},

for the 2-vectors are

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(V^{V}-\frac{2}{3}W^{V}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\Sigma^{V}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2a_{2}}\phi^{S}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}Q^{S}

(252)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{3}\left(3\dot{\Sigma}-2\dot{\Theta}+\frac{3\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{2a_{2}^{2}}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V},

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\Sigma^{V}+\overline{\Omega}^{V}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}V^{V}+\frac{2}{3}W^{V}-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\Sigma^{T}

(253)

\displaystyle-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}a^{V}-Q^{V},

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(X^{V}+\frac{1}{2}Y^{V}-\frac{1}{3}\mu^{V}\right)=\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{V}\right)-

(254)

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2a_{2}}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\phi^{S}-\left(2\dot{\mathcal{E}}+\dot{\Pi}-\frac{2}{3}\dot{\mu}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}+

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}\Sigma-\frac{1}{3}\Theta\right)\frac{1}{a_{2}}Q^{S},

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{V}\right)=\frac{1}{2}X^{V}+\frac{1}{4}Y^{V}+\frac{1}{3}\mu^{V}-

(255)

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)a^{V}-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T}\right)+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{3}\Theta+\frac{1}{4}\Sigma\right)Q^{V},

V^{V}-\frac{2}{3}W^{V}=2\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\Sigma^{T}-Q^{V},

(256)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{V}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\Sigma^{V}+\overline{\Omega}^{V}\right)

(257)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\zeta^{T}-\frac{1}{2a_{2}}\phi^{S},

and for the 2-tensors are

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}Q^{V}\right)=\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{1}{2a_{2}}Q^{S}

(258)

\displaystyle-\left(\mu+p-\frac{3\mathcal{E}}{2}+\frac{1}{4}\Pi\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}-\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\Sigma^{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{V}\right),

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\zeta^{T}=\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma^{T}+\frac{1}{a_{2}}a^{V}-\mathcal{E}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T},

(259)

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\Sigma^{T}=\frac{1}{a_{2}}\Sigma^{V}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\overline{\Omega}^{V}+\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\zeta^{T}.

(260)

E.2
Odd Parity

The evolution equations for the scalars are

2\dot{\xi}^{S}=\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\xi^{S}+\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\overline{\alpha}^{V}+\mathcal{H}^{S},

(261)

\dot{\Omega}^{S}=\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{S}+\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{2a_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V},

(262)

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{H}^{S}-\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{V}\right)

(263)

\displaystyle-3\left(\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\xi^{S},

for the 2-vectors are

\dot{\Omega}^{V}=-\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}+\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{V}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V},

(264)

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\mu}}^{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\mu}^{V}-\Theta\left(\overline{\mu}^{V}+\overline{p}^{V}\right)-(\mu+p)\overline{W}^{V}

(265)

\displaystyle+\dot{\mu}\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}-\frac{3}{2}\left(\Pi\overline{V}^{V}+\Sigma\overline{Y}^{V}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{X}}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{\overline{Y}}^{V}=2\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{X}^{V}-\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{6}\Pi\right)\overline{W}^{V}

(266)

\displaystyle+\left(\dot{\mathcal{E}}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{\Pi}\right)\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}-\frac{1}{3}\Theta Y^{V}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}(\mu+p-3\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi)\overline{V}^{V}-\frac{\Sigma}{2}\left(\overline{\mu}^{V}+\overline{p}^{V}\right),

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{V}}^{V}-\frac{2}{3}\dot{\overline{W}}^{V}=\frac{1}{3}\left(\overline{\mu}^{V}+3\overline{p}^{V}\right)-\overline{X}^{V}+\left(\dot{\Sigma}-\frac{2\dot{\Theta}}{3}\right)\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}

(267)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\overline{V}^{V}-\frac{2}{3}\overline{W}^{V}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\overline{Y}^{V},

\dot{\overline{a}}^{V}=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\overline{\alpha}^{V}-\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}+\overline{a}^{V}\right)+\mathcal{H}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{Q}^{V},

(268)

\dot{\overline{W}}^{V}=\dot{\Theta}\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\overline{\mu}^{V}+3\overline{p}^{V}\right)+\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}-\Theta\right)\overline{W}^{V}-3\Sigma\overline{V}^{V},

(269)

\dot{\overline{\Sigma}}^{V}=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}-\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}+\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\Sigma}^{V}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\alpha}^{V}-\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{V},

(270)

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\mathcal{E}}}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{\overline{\Pi}}^{V}=-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{V}+\frac{3}{4}\left(\Sigma-\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}+

(271)

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\Omega^{V}+\frac{3}{4a_{2}}\mathcal{H}^{S}-\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\overline{\alpha}^{V}+

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{4a_{2}}\mathcal{H}^{T}-\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{4a_{1}}\overline{Q}^{V}+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(3\mathcal{E}-2\mu-2p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\overline{\Sigma}^{V}-\left(\frac{1}{6}\Theta+\frac{1}{8}\Sigma\right)\overline{\Pi}^{V},

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{V}

\displaystyle=-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{V}\right)+\frac{3}{4}\left(\Sigma-\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{H}^{V}

(272)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}\left(2\overline{X}^{V}-\overline{Y}^{V}+\Sigma\overline{Q}^{V}\right)+\frac{3\mathcal{E}}{2}\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}\left(\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\overline{a}^{V}-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{4a_{2}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right),

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{Q}}^{V}+\overline{p}^{V}+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\overline{\Pi}^{V}=-\left(\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}

(273)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\overline{Y}^{V}-\frac{3}{2}\Pi\overline{a}^{V}-\left(\frac{4}{3}\Theta-\frac{1}{2}\Sigma\right)\overline{Q}^{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\overline{\Pi}^{T},

and for the 2-tensors are

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{T}

\displaystyle=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)+\frac{1}{a_{2}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{V}\right)-

(274)

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\left(\Sigma+\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{H}^{T}-\frac{3}{2}\left({\mathcal{E}}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\overline{\zeta}^{T},

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\mathcal{E}}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\dot{\overline{\Pi}}^{T}=\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\mathcal{H}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}(3\mathcal{E}+\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi)\overline{\Sigma}^{T}

(275)

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{3}{2}\Sigma+\Theta\right)\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2a_{2}}\overline{Q}^{V}-\frac{1}{6}\left(\Theta-\frac{3}{2}\Sigma\right)\overline{\Pi}^{T},

\dot{\overline{\zeta}}^{T}=\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\zeta}^{T}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\overline{\alpha}^{V}-\mathcal{H}^{T},

(276)

\dot{\overline{\Sigma}}^{T}=\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\Sigma}^{T}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}-\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}.

(277)

The constraints for the scalars are

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\xi^{S}=\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{S}+\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{2a_{2}}\overline{a}^{V},

(278)

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\Omega^{S}=\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\Omega^{V},

(279)

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{S}=\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\mathcal{H}^{V}-\left(\mu+p+3\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\Omega^{S}-\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\overline{Q}^{V},

(280)

for the 2-vectors are

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\mathcal{H}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{Q}^{V}\right)=\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\overline{\Sigma}^{V}-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\mathcal{H}^{T}-

(281)

\displaystyle\left(\mu+p-\frac{3\mathcal{E}}{2}+\frac{1}{4}\Pi\right)\Omega^{V}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{V}\right)+\frac{1}{2a_{2}}\mathcal{H}^{S},

\frac{\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\left(\Omega^{V}-\overline{\Sigma}^{V}\right)=3\Sigma\xi^{S}-\mathcal{H}^{S},

(282)

\displaystyle\overline{X}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{Y}^{V}+\frac{2}{3}\overline{\mu}^{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3}\left(\Sigma+\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{W}^{V}

(283)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\Theta}{3}-2\Sigma\right)\overline{V}^{V},

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\overline{V}^{V}-\frac{2}{3}\overline{W}^{V}\right)=\frac{2}{3}\left(3\dot{\Sigma}-2\dot{\Theta}\right)\Omega^{V},

(284)

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\overline{\Sigma}^{V}-\Omega^{V}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\overline{V}^{V}+\frac{2}{3}\overline{W}^{V}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\Omega^{S}

(285)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\overline{\Sigma}^{T}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{a}^{V}-\overline{Q}^{V},

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\overline{X}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{Y}^{V}-\frac{1}{3}\overline{\mu}^{V}\right)=\left(2\dot{\mathcal{E}}+\dot{\Pi}-\frac{2}{3}\dot{\mu}\right)\Omega^{V},

(286)

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{V}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\overline{X}^{V}+\frac{1}{4}\overline{Y}^{V}+\frac{1}{3}\overline{\mu}^{V}-

(287)

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\overline{a}^{V}+\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\mathcal{H}^{V}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{3}\Theta+\frac{1}{4}\Sigma\right)\overline{Q}^{V},

\overline{V}^{V}-\frac{2}{3}\overline{W}^{V}=\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k_{\perp}^{2}$}}{a_{2}}\overline{\Sigma}^{T}-2\mathcal{H}^{V}-\frac{2}{a_{2}}\Omega^{S}-\overline{Q}^{V},

(288)

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{V}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\overline{\Sigma}^{V}-\Omega^{V}\right)

(289)

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{2a_{2}}\overline{\zeta}^{T}+\frac{1}{a_{2}}\xi^{S},

and for the 2-tensors are

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\overline{\zeta}^{T}=\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\Sigma}^{T}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\overline{a}^{V}-\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T},

(290)

\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\overline{\Sigma}^{T}=-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\overline{\Sigma}^{V}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\Omega^{V}-\mathcal{H}^{T}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\zeta}^{T}.

(291)

E.3
Harmonic decomposition of equations for imperfect fluids

Harmonic decomposition of Eckart’s generalized equation

\tau_{q}\dot{q}^{\langle a\rangle}+q^{a}=-\kappa\left(D^{a}T+TA^{a}\right)\,,

(292)

yields

\tau_{q}\dot{Q}^{S}=-Q^{S}-\kappa\left(2a_{2}\dot{T}\overline{\Omega}^{V}+\frac{ia_{2}k_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}T^{V}+T\mathcal{A}^{S}\right)\,,\\

(293)

\displaystyle\tau_{q}\dot{Q}^{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\dot{Q}^{V}-\kappa\left(T^{V}+T\mathcal{A}^{V}\right)\,,

(294)

\displaystyle\tau_{q}\dot{\overline{Q}}^{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\overline{Q}^{V}-\kappa\left(\frac{2a_{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\dot{T}\Omega^{S}+T\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}\right)\,.

(295)

Similarly

\tau_{\pi}\dot{\pi}_{\langle ab\rangle}+\pi_{ab}=-2\eta\sigma_{ab}

(296)

gives

\tau_{\pi}\dot{\Pi}^{V}=-\Pi^{V}-2\eta\Sigma^{V},\;\tau_{\pi}\dot{\Pi}^{T}=-\Pi^{T}-2\eta\Sigma^{T}\,,

(297)

and

\tau_{\pi}\dot{\overline{\Pi}}^{V}=-\overline{\Pi}^{V}-2\eta\overline{\Sigma}^{V},\;\tau_{\pi}\dot{\overline{\Pi}}^{T}=-\overline{\Pi}^{T}-2\eta\overline{\Sigma}^{T}\,.

(298)

From (
296
) one also obtains the zeroth order equation

\tau_{\pi}\dot{\Pi}+\Pi+2\eta\Sigma=0\,,

(299)

for
\Pi
. The harmonic coefficients of its gradient
Y_{a}\equiv\delta_{a}\Pi
satisfies

\displaystyle\tau_{\pi}\dot{Y}^{V}=-\dot{\Pi}\left(\tau_{\pi\mu}\mu^{V}+\tau_{\pi{\cal{N}}}Z^{V}\right)+\frac{\tau_{\pi}}{2}\tilde{F}Y^{V}+

(300)

\displaystyle\tau_{\pi}\dot{\Pi}{\cal{A}}^{V}-Y^{V}-2\Sigma\left(\varsigma_{0}\mu^{V}+\varsigma_{1}Z^{V}\right)-2\eta V^{V}\,,

and

\overline{Y}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\Pi}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,,

(301)

respectively.
From the bulk viscosity equation

\tau_{\zeta}\dot{p}_{\zeta_{B}}+p_{\zeta_{B}}=-\zeta_{B}\Theta

(302)

follows that the harmonic coefficients of its gradient
p_{\zeta a}\equiv\delta_{a}p_{\zeta_{B}}
satisfies

\displaystyle\tau_{\zeta}\dot{p}_{\zeta}^{V}=-\dot{p}_{\zeta_{B}}\left(\tau_{\zeta\mu}\mu^{V}+\tau_{\zeta{\cal{N}}}Z^{V}\right)+\frac{\tau_{\zeta}}{2}\tilde{F}p_{\zeta}^{V}+

(303)

\displaystyle\tau_{\zeta}\dot{p}_{\zeta_{B}}{\cal{A}}^{V}-p_{\zeta}^{V}-\Theta\left(\beta_{0}\mu^{V}+\beta_{1}Z^{V}\right)-\zeta_{B}W^{V}\,,

and

\overline{p}_{\zeta}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{p}_{\zeta_{B}}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,,

(304)

respectively.
Similarly, from the continuity equation

0=\nabla_{a}{\cal{N}}^{a}=\dot{\cal{N}}+\Theta{\cal{N}}\,,

(305)

the harmonic coefficients of
Z_{a}\equiv\delta_{a}{\cal{N}}
are obtained from

\dot{Z}^{V}=\dot{\mathcal{N}}\mathcal{A}^{V}-\mathcal{N}W^{V}+\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}-\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)Z^{V}\,.

(306)

and

{\overline{Z}}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\cal{N}}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,

(307)

respectively. Finally the harmonic coefficients of the gradient of
T=T(\mu,{\cal{N}})
,
T_{a}\equiv\delta_{a}T
, are given by

T^{V}=\mathscr{T}_{0}\mu^{V}+\mathscr{T}_{1}Z^{V}\,,

(308)

and

\overline{T}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{T}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,,

(309)

respectively.
The coefficients above are given by

\displaystyle\varrho_{0}\equiv\frac{\partial\tilde{p}}{\partial\mu},\;\beta_{0}\equiv\frac{\partial\zeta_{B}}{\partial\mu},\;\varsigma_{0}\equiv\frac{\partial\eta}{\partial\mu},\;\mathscr{T}_{0}\equiv\frac{\partial T}{\partial\mu}\,,

\displaystyle\varrho_{1}\equiv\frac{\partial\tilde{p}}{\partial{\cal{N}}},\;\beta_{1}\equiv\frac{\partial\zeta_{B}}{\partial{\cal{N}}},\;\varsigma_{1}\equiv\frac{\partial\eta}{\partial{\cal{N}}},\;\mathscr{T}_{1}\equiv\frac{\partial T}{\partial{\cal{N}}}\,,

(310)

and

\tau_{\zeta\mu}\equiv\frac{\partial\tau_{\zeta}}{\partial\mu}\,,\quad\tau_{\zeta{\cal{N}}}\equiv\frac{\partial\tau_{\zeta}}{\partial{\cal{N}}}\,,\quad\tau_{\pi\mu}\equiv\frac{\partial\tau_{\pi}}{\partial\mu}\,,\quad\tau_{\pi{\cal{N}}}\equiv\frac{\partial\tau_{\pi}}{\partial{\cal{N}}}\,.

(311)

Appendix F
Harmonic Coefficients

From the equations in appendix
E
we can now solve for the even parity harmonics
\zeta^{T}
,
\mathcal{E}^{V}
,
\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}
,

\Sigma^{V}
,
\alpha^{V}
,
W^{V}
,
V^{V}
,
X^{V}
and
\phi^{S}
in terms of the seven even parity independent coefficients

\left\{\overline{\Omega}^{V},\mu^{V},\Sigma^{T},\mathcal{E}^{T},\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T},Q^{V},Q^{S}\right\}

(312)

and the freely specifiable function
p^{V}
,
\mathcal{A}^{V}
,

\mathcal{A}^{S}
,
\Pi^{V}
,
\Pi^{T}
and
Y^{V}
.

Similarly we solve for the odd parity harmonics
\overline{\zeta}^{T}
,
\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}
,
\mathcal{H}^{S}
,
\mathcal{H}^{V}
,

\overline{\Sigma}^{V}
,
\overline{\Sigma}^{T}
,
\overline{\alpha}^{V}
,
\overline{V}^{V}
,
\overline{W}^{V}
,
\overline{X}^{V}
,
\overline{Y}^{V}
,
\xi^{S}
,
\Omega^{V}
,

\overline{p}^{V}
and
\overline{\mu}^{V}
in terms of the four odd parity independent coefficients

\left\{\Omega^{S},\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T},\mathcal{H}^{T},\overline{Q}^{V}\right\}

(313)

and the freely specifiable functions
\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}
,

\overline{\Pi}^{V}
and
\overline{\Pi}^{T}
.

F.1
Even parity

The even harmonic coefficients can be expressed as

\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\zeta^{T}=\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma^{T}-\mathcal{E}^{T}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T},

(314)

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\left(\mathcal{E}^{V}+\frac{\Pi^{V}}{2}\right)=\frac{2ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}B}\left(\mu+p+\Pi\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}

(315)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma^{T}+\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}a_{2}B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)Q^{V}+

\displaystyle\left(\frac{3\mathcal{E}}{2}+\frac{3\Pi}{4}-\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}C}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{\Pi^{T}}{2}\right)+\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}a_{2}B}\mu^{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{6a_{1}}\left(2L+3\Sigma-2\Theta\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}^{2}B}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)Q^{S},

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\Sigma^{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\overline{\Omega}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(B+\frac{{\mathcal{R}}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right)\Sigma^{T}

(316)

\displaystyle+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left({\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T}\right)-\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}=

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\overline{\Omega}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{2k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\Sigma^{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left({\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi^{T}\right)-\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T},

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3a_{2}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{V}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2ia_{1}G}{3a_{2}k_{\parallel}B}\overline{\Omega}^{V}-\frac{\Sigma}{a_{2}B}\mu^{V}+\Sigma C\left({\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{\Pi^{T}}{2}\right)-

(317)

\displaystyle\left({\mathcal{E}}+\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\Sigma^{T}-\frac{ik_{\parallel}J}{3a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{ia_{1}}{3a_{2}^{2}k_{\parallel}B}\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right)Q^{S}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3a_{2}B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)-B\right)Q^{V},

\displaystyle\frac{2ik_{\parallel}}{3a_{1}a_{2}}\alpha^{V}=-\frac{2ia_{1}G}{3a_{2}k_{\parallel}B}\overline{\Omega}^{V}-\frac{\Sigma}{a_{2}B}\mu^{V}+\Sigma C\left({\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{\Pi^{T}}{2}\right)-

(318)

\displaystyle\left({\mathcal{E}}+\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\Sigma^{T}-\frac{ik_{\parallel}J}{3a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{ia_{1}}{3a_{2}^{2}k_{\parallel}B}\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right)Q^{S}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3a_{2}B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)-2B\right)Q^{V}+\frac{2}{3a_{2}}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{A}^{V},

\displaystyle\frac{W^{V}}{a_{2}}=\frac{ia_{1}}{k_{\parallel}a_{2}}\left(\frac{2k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}+\frac{G}{B}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(1-C\right)\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{\Pi^{T}}{2}\right)

(319)

\displaystyle-\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{2a_{1}}\left(2-J\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2a_{2}B}\mu^{V}+\frac{ia_{1}}{2a_{2}^{2}k_{\parallel}B}\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right)Q^{S}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2a_{2}B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)+2B\right)Q^{V}+\frac{1}{2}B(C-1)\Sigma^{T},

\displaystyle\frac{V^{V}}{a_{2}}=\frac{4ia_{1}}{3a_{2}k_{\parallel}}\left(\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}-\frac{G}{B}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}-\frac{B}{3}\left(1+2C\right)\Sigma^{T}+

(320)

\displaystyle\Sigma\left(1+2C\right)\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{\Pi^{T}}{2}\right)-\frac{2ik_{\parallel}}{3a_{1}}\left(1+J\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{2\Sigma}{a_{2}B}\mu^{V}

\displaystyle-\frac{2ia_{1}}{3a_{2}^{2}k_{\parallel}B}\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right)Q^{S}-\frac{2}{3a_{2}B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)-B\right)Q^{V},

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}^{2}}\phi^{S}=-\frac{4ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\left(\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\frac{\left(\frac{B}{2}+\mu+p+\Pi\right)}{B}-\frac{\Theta}{2}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}

(321)

\displaystyle-\frac{BL}{3}\Sigma^{T}+\left(2C\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}+\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right)\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{\Pi^{T}}{2}\right)-\frac{2ik_{\parallel}L}{3a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-

\displaystyle\frac{2k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}a_{2}B}\mu^{V}-\frac{2ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}a_{2}^{2}B}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)Q^{S}-\frac{2k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}a_{2}B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)Q^{V},

\displaystyle X^{V}+\frac{1}{2}Y^{V}=\left(\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{B}\left(\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}+3\mathcal{E}+\frac{3\Pi}{2}\right)\right)\mu^{V}+

(322)

\displaystyle\frac{ia_{1}}{k_{\parallel}B}\left(\mu+p+\Pi\right)\left(\tilde{B}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)-3\Sigma B\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}

\displaystyle+Ca_{2}\left(\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}+3\mathcal{E}+\frac{3\Pi}{2}\right)\left(\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{\Pi^{T}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle-a_{2}\left({\mathcal{E}}+\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\left(\Theta-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\right)\Sigma^{T}+

\displaystyle\frac{ia_{1}a_{2}}{6k_{\parallel}}\left(6\left(\Sigma-\frac{2}{3}\Theta\right)\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}-9\Sigma\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}+\tilde{B}L\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\tilde{B}-2B\right)Q^{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{ia_{1}}{2a_{2}k_{\parallel}B}\left(\tilde{B}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)-3\Sigma B\right)Q^{S},

where

B\equiv\tilde{k}^{2}+\frac{9\Sigma^{2}}{2}+3\mathcal{E}+\frac{3}{2}\Pi,

(323)

\tilde{B}\equiv\frac{4k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}+9\Sigma^{2}\,,

(324)

CB\equiv\Sigma\left(\Theta-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\right)-\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}},

(325)

G\equiv\left(\mu+p+\Pi\right)\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right),

(326)

LB\equiv 3\Sigma\left(\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{a_{2}^{2}}-\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)+\Theta\tilde{k}^{2},

(327)

JB\equiv\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}a_{1}^{2}}{k_{\parallel}^{2}a_{2}^{2}}\left({\mathcal{R}}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right)+2\Sigma\left(\Theta-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\right).

(328)

F.2
Odd parity

For the odd harmonic coefficients the following equations hold,

\Omega^{V}=\frac{ia_{2}k_{\parallel}}{a_{1}k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(329)

\overline{\mu}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\mu}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(330)

\overline{V}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\Sigma}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(331)

\overline{W}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\Theta}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(332)

\overline{X}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\mathcal{E}}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(333)

\overline{Y}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\Pi}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(334)

\overline{p}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{p}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(335)

\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\xi^{S}=\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{S},

(336)

\displaystyle\frac{ia_{2}k_{\parallel}}{a_{1}k_{\perp}^{2}}\mathcal{H}^{S}=-\frac{\mu+p+N}{\mu+p}\left(\frac{a_{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\left(\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\Omega^{S}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{Q}^{V}\right)

(337)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a_{2}B}\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}\right)\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\frac{B}{2}\overline{\Sigma}^{T}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\left(B+2\left(\mu+p\right)-\Pi\right)\Omega^{S}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)

(338)

\displaystyle+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\overline{Q}^{V},

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}^{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{a_{2}B}\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}\right)\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)

(339)

\displaystyle+\frac{a_{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\left(3\mathcal{E}-\frac{\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}}{\mu+p}N\right)\Omega^{S}-\frac{N}{2(\mu+p)}\overline{Q}^{V},

\displaystyle\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\overline{\alpha}^{V}=-\frac{a_{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\left(3\mathcal{E}-\frac{\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}}{\mu+p}N\right)\Omega^{S}+\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}

(340)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{a_{2}B}\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}\right)\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)+\frac{ik_{\parallel}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu+p+N}{2(\mu+p)}\overline{Q}^{V},

(341)

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}\right)}{2a_{2}}\left(\frac{a_{1}}{ik_{\parallel}}\left(1-\frac{9\Sigma^{2}}{2B}\right)\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)-\frac{3\Sigma}{B}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{ia_{1}a_{2}}{3k_{\parallel}k_{\perp}^{2}\Sigma}\left(\left(\mu+p+N\right)\left(\tilde{k}^{2}+3\mathcal{E}+\frac{3}{2}\Pi\right)\vphantom{\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}}+\frac{9\Pi\Sigma^{2}N}{4\left(\mu+p\right)}\right.

(342)

\displaystyle-\frac{a_{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\left(3\Sigma^{2}+\Sigma\Theta+\frac{\mu+p+N}{\mu+p}\left(\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)+\frac{k_{\parallel}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\Omega^{S}

\displaystyle\frac{B}{2}\overline{\zeta}^{T}

\displaystyle=-\frac{a_{1}}{ik_{\parallel}k_{\perp}^{2}}\left(B+2\left(\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{S}+

(343)

\displaystyle\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right){\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{a_{1}}{2ik_{\parallel}}\left(\tilde{k}^{2}-\mathcal{R}\right)\left(\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{1}{2}\overline{\Pi}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{ia_{1}}{k_{\parallel}a_{2}}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{Q}^{V}\,,

with

N\equiv\left(\mu+p\right)\left(1+\frac{2}{a_{2}^{2}B}\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-k_{\perp}^{2}\right)\right).

(344)

F.3
Extra harmonic coefficients in Eckart model

F.3.1
Even parity

p^{V}=\left(\varrho_{0}-\Theta\beta_{0}\right)\mu^{V}+\left(\varrho_{1}-\Theta\beta_{1}\right)Z^{V}-\zeta_{B}W^{V}\,,

(345)

\frac{1}{a_{2}}\mathcal{A}^{S}=-\frac{1}{\kappa T}\left(\frac{1}{a_{2}}Q^{S}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\kappa}{a_{1}}T^{V}+2\kappa\dot{T}\overline{\Omega}^{V}\right)\,,

(346)

\mathcal{A}^{V}=-\frac{1}{\kappa T}\left(Q^{V}+\kappa T^{V}\right)\,,

(347)

Y^{V}=-2\eta V^{V}-2\left(\varsigma_{0}\mu^{V}+\varsigma_{1}Z^{V}\right)\Sigma\,,

(348)

\Pi^{V}=-2\eta\Sigma^{V}\,,\;\,\Pi^{T}=-2\eta\Sigma^{T}\,,

(349)

T^{V}=\mathscr{T}_{0}\mu^{V}+\mathscr{T}_{1}Z^{V}\,.

(350)

F.3.2
Odd parity

\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}=-\frac{1}{\kappa T}\left(\overline{Q}^{V}+\kappa\frac{2a_{2}\dot{T}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\right)\,,

(351)

\overline{\Pi}^{V}=-2\eta\overline{\Sigma}^{V}\,,\;\overline{\Pi}^{T}=-2\eta\overline{\Sigma}^{T}\,,

(352)

\overline{p}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{p}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,,\;\overline{T}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{T}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,,

(353)

\overline{Y}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\Pi}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,,\;{\overline{Z}}^{V}=\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\cal{N}}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\Omega^{S}\,.

(354)
